# Claim ↔ source integrity audit

Generated by `content/tools/claim_source_audit.py` (deterministic, offline). Do not edit by hand; re-run the tool. Human reviews live in `content/tools/claim_source_reviews.json`.

Verdict = `reviewed` verdict if a ledger entry matches the claim text, otherwise the automatic verdict. `SUPPORTED` is reached only when every cited source is located (concrete locator) and was read in full / matched verbatim.
